[FPF C.40.OI and C.40.OC](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c40oi---bound-an-opportunity-inquiry) help bound the inquiry and characterize the subject and intended change. [PSDA.19](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/DECISION-DESIGN-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md#psda19---bound-the-development-direction-advising-engagement) bounds help when the adviser, recipient, sponsor and choice owner differ. Their result can be a better bounded inquiry or a qualified stop, not a compulsory programme.

Use [Human Capability Development (HCD)](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/Human%20Capability%20Engineering%20DPF%20Suite/HUMAN-CAPABILITY-DEVELOPMENT-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md) when a person must acquire the ability; use an adequate available service when supplied performance is sufficient. Neither route alone establishes self-development. Continue to the connected undertaking only when the receiving work needs that larger result.

#### Library.Reference:9.1.3 - Where does development begin, and what happens after success?

Development need not start with a chosen practice or an explicit problem. Recurrent workarounds, a surprising success, an avoided task or a narrowing of attainable possibilities can justify inquiry. The Core opportunity Methods [FPF C.40.OI, C.40.OC, C.40.NU, C.40.PC and C.40.DC](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c40oi---bound-an-opportunity-inquiry) connect such inquiry to a worthwhile problem, possible directions and the support they need. A known way can settle today's delivery. To design a continuing search, establish what the next practitioners must be able to vary, how they will distinguish useful alternatives and how an examination will affect retention or the next attempt. To account for past searches, recover the attempts actually made, including discarded ones where the question requires them.

In the **problem frame**, describe what happened and where the present way breaks down or a new possibility opens. **Problematisation** makes the difficulty, its conditions and the criterion for judging a proposed solution recoverable by the intended recipient. Follow the signal to the work affected. Who uses the result, what do they need to do with it, and where does the present arrangement hinder or enable that work? If several contributions meet there, examine their connection. Derive the criterion from the work and the conditions that govern it. Use the [ProblemCard (C.22.2:2.1)](FPF-Spec.md#c22221---thin-first-use-form) to state the grounded formulation and what its recipient can do with it. A sufficiently clear order can go directly to its performer. For a factual question, use an available source that covers the case. If a needed fact is missing, identify the record, observation or informant that could supply it. A research problem calls for knowledge still to be established. State what finding or argument would settle it. A choice between undertakings calls for a decision by those with authority to commit. A missing Method calls for a search or a construction. To choose a method, describe the task characteristics that affect eligibility or acceptance. If the repertoire covers different tasks with different methods, establish how the performer can recognize the task and choose a supported member in time. Once an applicable method or a supported way to choose it in time is available, formulate the task for a capable performer or role. State the required result, constraints, available means, authority and acceptance check for the work. [C.22 on task typing](https://fpf.tools/C.22:5) and [C.23 on family admission](https://fpf.tools/C.23:4.2).

**Strategizing** connects a worthwhile direction with ways of obtaining the result, the available performers and means, and the commitment justified by the comparison. A decision to fund a trial, retain a direction or stop can settle the strategic choice. **Development** changes what can be done or known and which further problems can be undertaken. A research sponsor can authorize a comparison of models. Researchers must do the work that establishes which predictions survive comparison with observations. [STR.18](DPFs/Management%20DPF%20Suite/STRATEGY-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md#str18---connect-strategic-reasoning--the-strategizing-mantra)

[FPF C.40.OD](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c40od---construct-and-continue-open-ended-development) connects choosing a transition, realizing it and continuing from the changed position. A Goldilocks problem is demanding enough to extend what is attainable and feasible with the available help, time and resources. Judge the value of the advance against the cost of an unsuccessful attempt. A short reversible trial and an irreversible change can warrant different choices.

When the desired result is settled and a way to obtain it is missing, use [FPF C.39](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c39---find-and-develop-a-way-to-obtain-a-result) to develop that way. Use C.40 when the result opens another opportunity worth pursuing and continued development is part of the undertaking. For improvement of the means of development, distinguish changing a Method or its provision from changing a holder's capability. Use E.23.CDI when a named holder must become more capable of conducting the development Work: identify its current limitation, choose an intervention and qualify the resulting capability in representative Work. A Method edit alone does not establish that change. An inquiry can finish with knowledge. For example, a research team that can now distinguish two previously indistinguishable predictions can test them where they diverge. A result that rules one out under the tested conditions is a scientific contribution in its own right.

[Section 9.4](#libraryreference94---from-a-thinking-repertoire-to-continuing-development) develops this application through a concrete thinking repertoire: how a recovered claim, new distinction, changed premise or constructed option becomes a useful contribution, and how that contribution can enter work, learning and continuation after success.

#### Library.Reference:9.1.4 - How do the different transformations form one undertaking?

Ordinary work, inquiry, changed subject capability, construction of products and Methods, support provision and cultural continuation must supply one another where the intended result depends on them.

Use [FPF C.40.OD:4](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c40od4---solution). It develops the typical connections and their conditional returns, including an adequate external contribution that changes which work the undertaking must perform itself. The result is a proposed connected undertaking with identifiable receiving contributions and conditions, not an already operating organization.

A transformation-flow network claim uses the distinct flow and network rules in [FPF E.18](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#e18---transformation-flow-structure) and [FPF E.18.NET](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#e18net---network-of-transformation-flow-structures). Methods, performed work, receiving dependencies and shared resources remain different relations. If only one adequate contribution is needed, there is no obligation to model a whole network. If a connection cannot be constructed from the supplied Methods, return that missing contribution to its actual practice.

#### Library.Reference:9.1.5 - Who should perform and support the Methods?

Compare complete arrangements, not office names. The same development work might be performed through ordinary managers and workplaces, a specialist development office, a common service for several masteries, a federation, an external provider or an extensive AI arrangement. Methods, capability, resources and powers must be connected without assuming that their structures coincide.

Suppose an engineering division and its supplier introduce a diagnostic method. For a decision about what to assign now, determine which judgements the division can make or obtain under its normal supplier agreement. A later fault or comparable previous work can supply that evidence. Assess a software agent at the configuration and with the tools intended for the assignment. For a claim that the division or agent acquired the capability, also establish what that same performer, with the relevant support, could do or obtain before. If its earlier capability is unknown, the current assignment can still be justified. A sound supplier report can establish the reported result. Which capability it helps demonstrate depends on whose work the commission concerns and which judgements that performer must make.

Personal development can also include delegated work. An engineer can use an AI assistant with an exocortex to retrieve past trials and suggest alternatives. When the aim is to learn to interpret observations, examine the person’s interpretation on a new case with the help intended for later use. Temporary coaching and a permanent assistant give different conditions for that judgement.

[FPF C.32.MWA.PP](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c32mwapp---construct-and-compare-method-performance-and-provision-arrangements) constructs these alternatives from the supplied practice and receiving result. It distinguishes the subject's necessary own contribution from substitutable provision, exposes joint demand and authority, and compares shared and specialized support. Its examples include a company without a new office, voluntary external participants and qualified AI. [Organization Change Engineering (OCE)](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/Management%20DPF%20Suite/ORGANIZATION-CHANGE-ENGINEERING-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md) supplies the organizational design and realization where an organization is the governed subject.

A tunnel-building company may need technical performance, learning and continued development. That does not decide that one office should own all three. A specialist development provider can obtain technical expertise from elsewhere while preserving its own strategic remit. Conversely, a common service can share common provision without treating every mastery as interchangeable. The Method compares these conditions and their whole costs.

To explain an existing practice, show how the contributors, methods and handovers together sustain the required work. For a design, give a realizable arrangement under the stated conditions. For a standby service, establish availability throughout the required window. For a report of delivery, establish what reached the recipient. To explain how a particular arrangement achieved it, trace the delivery through the connections that were used. Return to C.32.MWA.PP when a change in work, capacity, mandate or means affects that conclusion.

#### Library.Reference:9.1.6 - What help, learning and cultural continuation are needed?

These are different questions with different sufficient answers.

| Missing contribution | Useful entry and result | Ordinary boundary or return |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Help through which an interpretable practice becomes workable | [Implementation Facilitation (IS)](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/IMPLEMENTATION-FACILITATION-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md), IS.1–IS.3 and IS.5–IS.8: a bounded help relationship, diagnosis, support strategy and continuation or end; [FPF A.3.1.MI](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#a31mi---introduce-a-method-into-practice-and-decide-what-to-keep-or-change) supplies introduction, examination of later use and the decision to keep or change the Method | An unknown development direction returns to C.40.OI and the opportunity Methods; an unknown subject Method returns to its field or the general methodological constructions in Core. |
| A person must become capable, rather than receive a service | [Human Capability Development (HCD)](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/Human%20Capability%20Engineering%20DPF%20Suite/HUMAN-CAPABILITY-DEVELOPMENT-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md): learning purpose, prepared practice, feedback and transfer | Competent provider performance is not evidence of the recipient's learning; adequate existing competence may need only access or support. |
| Useful participation, relationships or joint contributions | [Community Management (CB)](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/Management%20DPF%20Suite/COMMUNITY-MANAGEMENT-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md), starting at CB.1 or the known missing Method | Do not create a community when independent use of an available resource is enough. Employment does not by itself establish community participation or voluntary mandate. |
| A practice must continue among further carriers, including independent people, collectives or AI | [FPF C.36](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c36---cultural-evolution-and-cultural-evolution-engineering) and [FPF C.36.RP](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c36rp---sustain-and-renew-shared-ways-of-working), with the subject practice's sources | Transmission or participation counts do not establish reconstruction, use or capacity to continue. Follow the cultural Method even when there is no community organizer. |

In an exercise on problem formulation, the teacher may give the learner material from one episode. If recognizing an occasion for development is part of the task, let the learner select the material as well. For independent formulation, leave the choice of problem and method open. The learner may identify a difficulty or see an opportunity created by earlier success. Ask for a thin ProblemCard and give the material and card to a reviewer, a colleague or a qualified software agent. Can they tell who would use the result, what it would let that person do and what in the material warrants the question? For a difficulty, the criterion should distinguish an improvement from a change that leaves the difficulty in place. For a new opportunity, it should distinguish the proposed gain from simply continuing successful work. If the reviewer has to guess, revise the formulation. A card can also fulfil a narrower task to express or justify a supplied problem. Credit the choices the learner actually made. An exercise in formulation can finish before a method has been found.

Use that formulation in a second exercise. The learner finds or constructs a method, compares its expected result with the criterion, and specifies an executable task. Where a known method already fits, they can go straight to the task. For transfer, use a different episode and let the learner initiate the formulation. A singer who can hold pitch with accompaniment, for instance, might choose to record an unaccompanied passage and use the score to locate where pitch drifts. The recording shows what the singer performed under the recorded conditions. To establish who chose this test, use evidence tied to that decision, such as the singer’s plan recorded before the teacher suggested an answer. For a decision about the singer’s ability to frame such a problem, comparable previous work can already show that they made the choice independently.

For a culture of development, combine the cultural continuation contribution with C.40.OD's development result. Reproducing a training activity or one mastered Method does not show that further carriers can notice a consequential difficulty or possibility, construct a worthwhile development problem, and organize the next attainable transition. A local helping undertaking can nevertheless finish before that larger culture claim is established.

#### Library.Reference:9.1.7 - How does a useful episode become a renewable cultural contribution?

Make the reason for the way recoverable, not just the appearance of the successful example. [FPF C.36.MC:4.5](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c36mc45---make-a-practice-recoverable-and-changeable-by-its-recipients) connects receiving work, the explanation of consequential choices, bounded adaptation, recipient use and the return of a useful difference. [FPF C.36.RP](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c36rp---sustain-and-renew-shared-ways-of-working) obtains and renews the means of using a shared way, whether through material, capable help, tools, learning or another arrangement.

Within a community, [CB.14](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/Management%20DPF%20Suite/COMMUNITY-MANAGEMENT-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md#cb14---develop-and-maintain-a-shared-repertoire-of-practice) turns contrasting cases into a qualified repertoire contribution and makes it available beyond its original contributors. Determine whether an observed difference calls for changing the Method, its explanation, a tool, access or the participant's capability; involve the contributor able to make that change. A successful local variant can be worth retaining even when the earlier way remains sound elsewhere.

Keep reconstruction, useful performance, adaptation and later retention as separate claims. A public resource may reach independent carriers who never join a meeting. Conversely, repeated participation may leave a necessary operation unavailable. Keep only the observations needed for the intended claim and obtain further evidence when its possible contribution warrants the burden.

For a culture of continuing development, what another carrier must reconstruct includes recognising a consequential difficulty, framing a worthwhile problem and organising an attainable next transition. [FPF C.40.NP](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c40np---continue-from-a-result-to-new-attainable-problems) develops that continuation after success. If the next carrier can only repeat the founder's prepared exercise, their ability to choose and address a new problem remains unestablished.

#### Library.Reference:9.1.8 - What should be kept, revised or stopped?

Compare the result with the intended benefit and conditions of use. Delivered assistance, acquired mastery, changed organisational capability, useful participation and cultural continuation need their own grounds. [CB.10](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/Management%20DPF%20Suite/COMMUNITY-MANAGEMENT-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md#cb10---understand-the-value-of-participation-and-cultural-work) examines the value of participation and cultural work; [FPF C.32.MWA.PP:4.10](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c32mwapp410---change-the-arrangement-while-preserving-its-valid-uses) follows a changed condition through the affected provision arrangement. Course attendance or a lower complaint count cannot alone settle these different claims.

Retain supported uses when another use fails. A lost permission may block one shared case without invalidating the Method. A provider's departure may defeat timely help while the material remains adequate. Repair the affected contribution, change the promise or obtain another provider. [IS.5](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/IMPLEMENTATION-FACILITATION-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md#is5---sustain-transfer-extend-or-end-implementation-support) handles continuation or withdrawal of implementation support; [CB.11](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/Management%20DPF%20Suite/COMMUNITY-MANAGEMENT-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md#cb11---continue-change-transfer-or-end-a-community-arrangement) handles the community arrangement's continuation, transfer or end.

A support engagement can end when the recipient has the agreed result. If that result opens a worthwhile possibility, the participants can keep it for later or pass it to someone able to pursue it. When continuing development is part of their work, use C.40.NP to choose a problem the recipient can now take on. Consolidation, rest or ending an exhausted branch may make that choice possible later.

#### Library.Reference:9.1.9 - What changes when capable AI becomes inexpensive?

Recompare the whole arrangement. A qualified agent may supply inquiry, source recovery, construction, explanation, practice feedback or an authorized decision. The changed viable division of work can remove a specialist bottleneck or an unnecessary office.

Use [C.32.MWA.PP's AI case (FPF C.32.MWA.PP:5.6)](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c32mwapp56---capable-ai-changes-the-whole-arrangement-not-just-the-coachs-speed) to compare complete performance and support arrangements under the changed AI capability. Use [FPF C.11.DUA](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c11dua---make-advice-and-evidence-demands-worth-their-burden) to judge what the advice to change that arrangement, or its demands for further inquiry and checking, can obtain relative to their full burden. Include the capability, access, context, powers, qualification, shared capacity and consequences of error. Remove human duplication only when the replacement supplies the contribution on which the receiving result depends. Do not retain people merely because an earlier arrangement had them; do not assume a general capability claim supplies the required local judgement or authorization.

If the intended gain is a human's mastery or self-development, AI performing all of the work can defeat that gain even when the immediate output improves. If the gain is an externally supplied result, requiring the human to learn the provider's Method may add waste. Return to the intended result, then change the performance and support arrangement. The worked costs in these Methods are prospective models, not measured universal savings.

#### Library.Reference:9.1.10 - How does the selected practice become usable locally?

To realize a selected proposal, the responsible performers need usable Methods, means and powers, sufficient preparation, commitments and an entry into real work.

Follow [FPF C.32.MWA.PP:4.9](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c32mwapp49---give-the-selected-realization-its-contribution-and-conditions) to the missing contribution. [FPF A.15.8.MS](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#a158ms---configure-methodological-support-for-work) makes Method material usable; [FPF A.3.1.MI](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#a31mi---introduce-a-method-into-practice-and-decide-what-to-keep-or-change) introduces the Method and examines later use; OCE.9 realizes an organizational change; Implementation Facilitation provides and adapts the helping undertaking; HCD develops a person's needed capability. An adequate already available contribution ends its branch. A changed result or dependency returns to the supplying Method and the affected whole.

Use [FPF E.4.DPF](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#e4dpf---domain-and-local-principle-frameworks-whether-and-how-to-author-and-publish) when a bounded local practice needs an LPF language. Keep local decisions and examples distinct from the general Method, retain its supplier and conditions, and revise local guidance when those conditions change. A hierarchy of projects does not imply a hierarchy of copied frameworks. A service description, programme, assignment or architecture decision remains its own kind of document.

#### Library.Reference:9.1.11 - From an available result to a useful return

C.40.OI bounds the inquiry; C.40.OC makes the subject's relevant characteristics, current readings, intended changes and unknowns explicit. C.40.NU varies uses, technologies, characteristics and supports. C.40.PC asks what worthwhile receiving problem the proposed contribution would answer. C.40.DC keeps that contribution with the configuration needed to obtain it.

Enter at the first missing result. A team with a sound inquiry can search without repeating intake. A practitioner with a qualified direction can examine its joint conditions through [FPF C.40.JF](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c40jf---qualify-dependencies-and-joint-reachability). [FPF C.40.OP](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c40op---maintain-opportunities-and-stepping-stones) can retain useful possibilities and conditional enabling relations without selecting a programme or committing resources. If responsible advice is wanted, use the missing service or Decision Design contribution.

This division preserves useful work at several stopping points. An inquiry can end with a better question. A direction can remain useful while one permission or support result is missing. A retained path can name a later possibility without asserting that its first step happened. Identify who is authorized to make each consequential choice and who is authorized to carry out the intervention. Those assignments can belong to different agents.

When the needed result is a continuing development undertaking, carry these returns into C.40.OD. It connects them with the professional work that can realize and qualify the change. When independently worthwhile undertakings draw on common means, STR.16 constructs the joint portfolio and compares support arrangements; Strategy still supplies strategic commitment and the holders of authority make the decisions. Neither addition changes the finite result or non-use boundary of the opportunity and advising Methods.

#### Library.Reference:9.1.12 - Qualify the combination as well as its ingredients

Joining results requires three judgements: each result is adequately qualified; it fits the receiving subject, configuration, use and horizon; and the proposed combination meets its shared conditions. A sound result for another data class or support arrangement may fail the second judgement. Individually feasible directions can fail the third.

For the combined use, establish the receiving contribution, compatible premises, jointly available resources and permissions, protected conditions and the remaining uncertainty. Identify the next responsible receiver for a missing result. Reuse the detailed qualifications already supplied by the patterns and direct practices; a new combined demand, audience, configuration or horizon needs its own answer. Recognition means that a reader can recover this account. Assurance requires the measurements, domain evidence, competence, permissions and other qualified results on which it relies.

In C.40.JF's constructed eight-week case, directions A and B each require the same specialist for two hours every week; only two hours are available after existing obligations. Each direction fits alone. Together they need four hours and fail the joint condition under the no-overtime and urgent-cover protections. Doing A and then B takes sixteen weeks, outside the eight-week horizon. Shared support would be another configuration whose combined demand needs qualification. Preserve the individual directions and the joint limit. A combined-work estimate, alternative-support result, or revised-horizon result becomes a request only when its obtainable contribution warrants acquisition.

[FPF C.40.JF:5.4](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c40jf54---all-pairs-fit-but-the-whole-combination-does-not) distinguishes pairwise from whole feasibility; ruling out the full combination leaves the choice of a subset open.

**Constituent actions in ongoing work.** During programme advising, estimating a participant's available practice time can be part of comparing the feasibility of directions, which is itself part of constructing advice for that participant. If the intended programme requires coordinated practice with a partner, solitary free time is no longer the same usable resource. Arithmetic skill and knowledge of the desired profession can coexist with a missing ability to construct a feasible practice arrangement. Recover that intermediate contribution and its support before recommending the programme. B.1.5.EW supplies the general vertical question; the domain and HCD supply the requisite Methods and capability account. A feasible single step does not establish the feasibility of the advised combination.

#### Library.Reference:9.1.13 - Architectural rationale

Start from the result the recipient commissioned. Identifying a worthwhile opportunity or giving advice can finish an inquiry. Bringing about a change also requires the contributions to work together and the necessary work to take place. Choosing a portfolio across independent purposes calls for decisions about shared support and competing demands. These tasks can arise in the same organization. If composition matters, identify which Method is required or admitted as a constituent of the encompassing Method, or which performance actually formed part of the undertaking. Previously supplied advice can guide a new undertaking even when its preparation took place outside it.

The opportunity and advising Methods retain opportunity construction and advice because their results are reused in the larger practice and can be sought independently. C.40.OD supplies the connected development construction; its subject-specific learning, engineering, inquiry, cultural and organization work use their professional suppliers. STR.16 supplies the joining construction for independent purposes, shared support, resource competition and commitments. Retaining a possibility in C.40.OP does not allocate it, and comparing a human programme in HCD.2 does not construct an organization's portfolio of research, products and development undertakings.

Using Decision Design alone remains a simpler alternative for a recommendation question with adequate inputs. HCD, OCE, Strategy or another professional Method can likewise finish their own question directly. A list of those suppliers is insufficient when the reader still has to invent how their outputs become one feasible development undertaking or how the first success opens a different worthwhile question. The connected Methods make that joining work explicit without absorbing the supplying disciplines.

The connected repertoire is broader than one composite Method. A framework may offer several independently reusable ways whose results constrain one another; its publication Parts are not Method constituents or a mandatory action order. A supplied domain result is a premise under qualified conditions, not the domain's entire Method.

The opportunity, advice and continuing-practice contributions are independently useful. This architectural reference connects the complete Core patterns and domain-framework publications so that a practitioner can discover and use their joins while retaining PatternIDs. A separate publication becomes preferable when a branch has an independently useful whole and the revised distribution serves its use, learning and change better. Reconsider the organization when changed uses or contributions make another arrangement more useful.

A useful narrower profile can inherit applicable guidance and explain its changed situation, contribution, conditions and evidence needs. More than one level of narrowing is possible, and a contribution can serve several profiles. State whether the relation is bounded-use projection, specialization, composition, reuse or result dependence.

#### Library.Reference:9.1.14 - Covered work and independent entry

The covered work is conducting and improving a named subject's development, including self-development when that subject contributes to conducting its own development. Recurring questions include unnoticed difficulty, an unformed aspiration, construction of a worthwhile problem, reachable directions and means, joint provision, enactment, actual changed possibilities and continuation after success. Ordinary work, implementation, advice, learning and Method creation can each contribute; none alone defines the whole.

C.40.OD constructs the connected undertaking using the qualified subject and professional results it needs. STR.16 constructs a feasible portfolio for an organization or provider with independently worthwhile undertakings and shared means. Opportunity construction uses C.40.OI, C.40.OC and C.40.NU, C.40.PC, C.40.DC, C.40.JF and C.40.OP without requiring an adviser. Advising can start with a formed question, qualified opportunity or existing HCD.2 programme comparison. Shared inquiry, characterization, joint conditions and changed-premise qualification remain results to obtain when missing.

The supplying Core and domain-framework publications contain the complete pattern bodies, their material connections and heterogeneous worked uses. This architectural reference connects those contributions. The direct supplier result and conditions remain part of the receiving construction. If that result is unavailable, keep the gap.

[PSDA.Application:4](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/DECISION-DESIGN-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md#psdaapplication4---app-psd-04--miras-development-direction-programme-comparison-and-guide) follows a person-to-Guide use, [PSDA.Application:5](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/DECISION-DESIGN-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md#psdaapplication5---app-psd-05--an-engineering-companys-next-development-opportunities) an organization inquiry, and [PSDA.Application:6](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/DECISION-DESIGN-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md#psdaapplication6---app-psd-06--unlike-subjects-exact-recipients-and-an-anti-case) unlike subjects. C.40.OD adds a connected undertaking with two substantive development transitions and changed performing arrangements. STR.16 adds a provider portfolio, a decision-reversing resource change and an external research partner. These are teaching cases, not an empirical coverage claim or the Method's scope boundary.

#### Library.Reference:9.1.15 - Results supplied by other practices

The publications below indicate ways to obtain the results. For the receiving operation, establish which result is already available, what someone must still do and who can make any required decision effective.

| Missing contribution or current question | Where to find a method and what its use can yield | Qualification and receiving effect |
| --- | --- | --- |
| An examinable change before its future application is settled | [FPF C.40](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c40---develop-branching-search-from-reusable-material): examined material and a worthwhile continuation or stop. [FPF C.40.CD](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c40cd---develop-problems-and-ways-of-solving-them-together) when problems and ways of answering them develop together. | A suggested application enters C.40.NU or C.40.PC as a lead or hypothesis. A local result can be useful without establishing demand, a development opportunity or a qualified future path. |
| One specialist premise | A.15.9 with the supplying practice for an existing qualified result, a required or worthwhile new contribution, or a remaining gap. | Preserve subject, configuration, use, scope and window. Establish who selects the Method, who must reach the conclusion and who can decide on its use under the commission. |
| Characteristic meaning or measurement | The relevant subject practice supplies the characteristic's meaning and bearer; A.17 resolves unclear Characteristic, Scale or reading distinctions. Use C.16 to construct a measurement model or interpret a performed measurement. | C.40.OC selects what matters to this inquiry and may return a useful characterization with an explicit unknown. A model can supply a conditional result before measurement. For a performed measurement, retain its subject, Scale, applicable unit, method, model, required calibration basis, uncertainty, time basis and actual work under C.16. Comparing measured values requires compatible measurement grounds. |
| Comparison of a finite change | C.11.CRC and the relevant domain practice. Compare what would happen over the same horizon and external scenarios if the current configuration `S0` continued or the feasible change to `S1` were made. | Identify who would obtain each result or bear each cost, protected conditions, interactions, transition, reversibility, later options and evidence limits. State which comparison the evidence supports and which consequences could still reverse it. |
| Human demand, capability or development | [HCD.1](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/Human%20Capability%20Engineering%20DPF%20Suite/HUMAN-CAPABILITY-DEVELOPMENT-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md#hcd1--derive-human-capability-demand-from-representative-later-work) for the requirements of later work, including a provisional account before that work begins; [HCD.3](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/Human%20Capability%20Engineering%20DPF%20Suite/HUMAN-CAPABILITY-DEVELOPMENT-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md#hcd3--diagnose-whether-a-capability-misconception-or-behaviour-limits-work) for causes of an observed difficulty; the relevant HCD and target-domain Methods for support, practice and transfer. | Use evidence about the relevant person under compatible conditions. General audience assumptions leave individual fit to be established; performance with help leaves independent performance or transfer to unfamiliar tasks to be established. |
| Human programme alternatives | HCD.2 compares programmes, returning a feasible candidate within its limits or identifying the design question or obstacle that prevents one. | Compare demands on the learner and provider, access, ability to perform the whole task, displaced work and uncertainty. Programme advice and authorized choice remain separate. |
| Engagement, alternatives, comparison or recommendation | Use an applicable engagement, comparison or recommendation already in hand. Where one is missing, use PSDA.11 to agree the engagement with its participants, PSDA.5–PSDA.9 to construct and compare alternatives, or PSDA.14 to develop the recommendation. | Keep the intended recipient, use and uncertainty explicit. Choose a workshop only when it serves that work. Before running a recommended probe, establish the permission or rule that authorizes it and whether its conditions have been met. An authorized recommendation can itself give permission when the applicable rule makes its issue sufficient. |
| Advice follow-up | PSDA.15: feasible observation, interpretation and affected recommendation return. | Distinguish earlier advice, actual choice, implementation and evidence. A missing necessary observation is not a favourable result. |
| Organizational arrangement, strategic direction or continuing operation | Use OCE when participants need to change their organizational arrangement, STR to develop direction and commitments, SYSE for realizing the required system, and OPS for operating choices. | Obtain the result needed by the receiving operation. Reuse an existing comparison of arrangements when it already answers that question. |
| Guide realization | The Learning Product Development LPF: LG.02 designs a learning route, LG.03 checks whether a Guide fragment supports the learner's next action, and LG.05 develops a unit that teaches use of a pattern. | Begin realization from an authorized programme choice. An authorized choice may be recorded in the Guide itself. To establish learning, reduced load or transfer, use evidence for that outcome, including a learner’s own work if the Guide records it. The published exercise alone establishes what readers were asked to do. [Source access and qualification limits (PSDA.Sources:4.1)](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/DPFs/DECISION-DESIGN-PRINCIPLES-FRAMEWORK.md#psdasources41---learning-product-source-access) determine which full instructional source or qualified result must be obtained. |
| Professional service conditions | The applicable career, consulting or other professional practice: established competence, participation, confidentiality, conflict treatment, disclosure, referral and closure conditions. | Establish which professional conditions apply to the engagement and what local requirements it must meet. Codes and service forms do not establish personal competence, permission or a universal legal rule. |
| AI, engineered, cultural, research, legal, safety, security or financial premise | The subject practice: the named evaluation, relation, criterion, authority, constraint or evidence result. | Retain the exact object and receiving conditions. Missing or stale results produce a bounded request, narrower claim, blocker or abstention, not a cross-domain analogy. |
| Several uses affected by one changed source claim | A.10.1: needed bounded discovery, actual dependence and coverage, followed by the supported direct subject result or scoped gap. A sufficient direct return needs no additional common account. | A citation alone is not reliance; an inaccessible or uninspected receiver is not unaffected. For one known reliance, use the direct subject guidance and retain the support needed for that use. Apply A.10 when the source-to-use account remains implicit or must be replayable. |
| Source or guidance currentness | G.11 and the responsible maintainer of the source or framework: currentness, decay, scoped refresh and reporting. | A recent date or refresh completion does not create the new domain result, edition admission, access or effectiveness. |
| Construction and change of the connected development undertaking | B.5.PI to open inquiry from ordinary work, C.40.PC to formulate a worthwhile problem and C.22.2 to check its formulation. C.39 for a missing way; C.40.CD for developing problems and ways of solving them together; C.40.CU for constructing a useful and reproducible application; C.32.MWA for distinct Method, Work and provision structures. | C.40.OD supplies their development-practice connection. A supplier's conceptual result is not evidence of actual performance, acquired capability or an obtaining transformation-flow network. |
| Research, strategic and resource contributions to a portfolio | RMP.1, RMP.2 and RMP.8; STR.8, STR.11 and STR.14; MA.1–MA.3 and MA.8; OCE.8, OCE.9 and OCE.11: independently justified inquiry, contribution construction, bounded commitment, shared-demand accounting and realization. | STR.16 joins these qualified results without deciding scientific truth, resources or client authority by itself. Independent epistemic work need not be a prerequisite for an existing product. |
| Cultural continuation and useful participation | C.36 and C.36.RP for cultural reconstruction, use, retention and renewed carriers; Community Management for actual participation arrangements; the target practice supplies the content. | A cultural population need not be one interacting community or organization. Participation, access and independently reproduced capability require different results. |

#### Library.Reference:9.1.16 - Requalification and guidance refresh

For a changed client fact, configuration, support, use, horizon or service condition, use C.40.RQ to find the smallest affected opportunity result. Preserve independent qualified uses, historical advice and actual prior configurations. A narrowed data class needs its own quantities and comparisons; a missed necessary observation limits only the reliance that needed it.

For a changed development undertaking, C.40.OD reconnects the affected receiving work, provision and continuation. For a changed shared contribution, timing or allocation, STR.16 reconstructs its affected portfolio uses without inventing dependencies for independent branches. Use C.40.RQ for the opportunity claims whose premises changed; it need not absorb the whole enactment or resource decision.

For a changed source claim with several receiving uses, A.10.1 supplies bounded discovery and actual dependence before each direct practice qualifies its result. For source currentness, decay or scoped refresh, use G.11. A moved file with unchanged accessible claims can require only a repaired reference; an uninspected receiver or expired reliance window cannot be declared unchanged for convenience.

When a source or practitioner result defeats the guidance itself, identify the failed move, receiving use, source difference and candidate repair. Return that account to the authorized recipient. When framework maintenance or referral is part of the undertaking, take the finding to the responsible maintainer through the permitted route. Change only the dependent body, relation, entry or example unless the field or branch boundary is defeated. Updating a client account is not automatically a framework revision; updating a publication date is not a new qualification.

### Library.Reference:9.2 - Help, enablement and the organization of professional support

A capable person can be unable to act because the needed information, time or authority is missing. Another person can obtain the same result from an expert without acquiring the expert's skill. A third wants to learn but needs practice and useful feedback. A team may need a usable offer or a way to resolve conflicting commitments. Several capable performers may simply be carrying out their respective shares of a common undertaking. When considering help, establish whose wanted or legitimately undertaken result is at issue and what would make it obtainable. Identify who could supply any missing work or provision. Learning is one possible aim.

This account connects the Library's Methods for obtaining contributions, developing capability, introducing practices and organizing their provision. It is for a practitioner arranging help with professional work: a recipient, colleague, leader, teacher, service provider or organizer of an office. It explains how their contributions fit together. The professional content remains necessary: this account does not teach metrology, project management, teaching reading or the technical discipline an office serves.

The same arrangement can serve a quality culture, project management, agile work, scientific reasoning, literacy, engineering or another professional practice. An office of development is a thematic application. A team without an office can use the same distinctions. Begin with the result someone wants to obtain. Help can address a present difficulty or make a first undertaking possible. Use a familiar service directly when it supplies what the recipient needs.

#### Library.Reference:9.2.1 - Different changes can make the next use possible

Help is a contribution intended to make a recipient's wanted or otherwise legitimately undertaken result obtainable. Its intended benefit and its actual effect are different. The recipient's account of the difficulty, the provider's diagnosis and a sponsor's request can disagree. Establish whose result is at issue and which claims each can make before selecting an intervention.

Locate the contribution still needed. If the helper performed an operation perfectly, would that fulfil the undertaking or would it leave part of the recipient’s work undone? For a delivery, the provider can perform the whole operation. For participation, establish what opportunity the recipient should have, what action is theirs and who may represent them. For capability development, establish which judgements or actions the intended holder needs to become able to perform, with the help available in later work. An existing capability may suffice for a new undertaking once a missing condition is supplied.

Investigate causes or limiting conditions when resolving them would change the choice of assistance. Keep unresolved explanations open when they support the same adequate contribution. An unsatisfactory result alone does not establish lack of skill.

Allocate the actual operations and the means, capacity, participation and rights they require. Obtain a sufficient existing contribution directly; construct or compare arrangements when the allocation remains open. Examine what the supplied contribution enables in the receiving work. A delivered result, changed capability and repaired provision can coexist and need their respective grounds. Continue, adapt or end the help according to the remaining use.

These connected operations can be entered where a result is missing. They do not require a new interview or plan when an existing service already meets the need. Five common results help discriminate the choice:

| Needed change | What would be obtained | A misleading substitute |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A contribution to current work | A usable analysis, action, decision input or completed operation | An explanation that leaves the required operation undone |
| Role entry or participation | An effective appointment, a usable opportunity to contribute, a participant’s own undertaking or their actual contribution, according to the governing terms | Treating an invitation as evidence that the participant gave a required judgement |
| The recipient's own capability | The recipient can perform the required class of work under its intended conditions | A correct product supplied by somebody else |
| Available provision | Time, information, equipment, assistance, access or authority becomes usable when needed | A resource listed in a catalogue but unavailable at the moment of use |
| A changed way or shared practice | A better Method, explanation, coordination arrangement or continuing repertoire | Repeated encouragement to follow an unsuitable way |

These results can coexist. An expert and novice can jointly complete today's work while the novice learns. Their shared hour is one expenditure, but the work and learning claims require different grounds. A repaired access arrangement can make existing skill usable without changing that skill. A new team procedure can alter collective capability without making every participant able to perform every role.

C.32.MWA.PP supplies the comparison of whole configurations of performance and provision. C.40.JD supplies joint development when a way, its holder and its provision must change together. IS.3 supplies construction and adaptation of support for establishing, using or revising a target practice. LDR.1 supplies work on participation and role contribution. Use the result from each Method; their names are not a sequence that every request must traverse.

Consider a project planner who submits a poor forecast. Before enrolling the planner in training, examine the receiving decision, forecast construction and conditions. Missing supplier dates call for information or an explicitly qualified estimate. A misunderstood dependency may call for explanation and practice. An unrealistic commitment imposed by the sponsor calls for work on the commitment. A sound forecast rejected because it is unwelcome calls for an accountable disagreement. The same visible defect does not establish the same cause.

#### Library.Reference:9.2.2 - Identify recipient and helper contributions

Receiving help may require work by the recipient: identifying a difficulty, supplying inputs, choosing among offers or using a returned contribution. Establish the operations needed for the particular result. The helper performs or arranges a contribution to that result. Identify which operations each participant performs; they may use the same Method or different Methods. A participant that benefits from supplied work need not have performed that work. Neither position has complete access to the other's knowledge, constraints or intentions.

A first-person account therefore differs from the helper's account:

| Moment | Recipient's operative question | Helper's operative question | What an observer can establish |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Recognizing the need | What am I trying to obtain, and what can I already do or obtain? | What is the recipient’s work, and where could my contribution help? | The intended result, what is already available and any grounds for locating a difficulty |
| Choosing help | Do I need an answer, participation, my own capability or better conditions? What am I willing and able to contribute? | Which contribution can I supply, and which professional judgement is still needed? | Whether the proposed contribution matches the receiving use and has a feasible provider |
| Agreeing the engagement | What do I accept, retain authority over, provide and get to question? | What do I undertake, need from others and return if the conditions fail? | The act or condition that establishes an undertaking or permission under the applicable terms, and any choice still open |
| Working | What must I do myself, what can I use from the helper, and when should I ask again? | When should I explain, demonstrate, perform, ask, provide feedback or change the arrangement? | Actual actions, support and results, with the relevant division of work |
| Continuing | What can I now do under the next conditions? What help remains useful? | Has this engagement supplied its result, and what continuing provision is justified? | Supported continuation, unresolved result or changed condition; no automatic claim of personal mastery |

The table distinguishes whose action and access the account concerns. One participant can switch positions, and reciprocal help can connect several such relations. Self-help can combine the positions without eliminating the need to distinguish a desired result from evidence that it was obtained.

Familiar role pairs recover different parts of this allocation:

| Role wording | Contribution to identify | What remains to establish |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Learner–teacher, when capability development is intended | The learner is the holder of the capability being developed; the teacher supplies specified teaching contributions. | The learner's own operations, the supplied explanation, demonstration or feedback, conditions of later work and evidence of acquisition. |
| Worker–helper | The worker performs the named target work; the helper supplies a contribution to it or its conditions. | Constituent or joint operations, supplied results, repaired provision and what the worker still does. A recipient need not be the target worker. |
| Performer–consultant | The performer executes a named operation; the consultant supplies a professional contribution under the engagement's conditions. | Who receives and uses the advice or service, what diagnosis, performance, recommendation or capability development is included, and who holds the relevant decisions. |

One participant can use several Methods, and several participants can use the same Method. A teacher can consult and a consultant can teach when that contribution is wanted, competent and permitted. The names do not by themselves establish competence, an assignment or a decision right.

Other learning claims need their own interpretation under E.10.LRN. Gaining a fact, revising an episteme or training a technical model does not, merely through a learner or teacher title, establish acquisition of a capability for later work. Learning can also occur through observing ordinary work without the observed participant undertaking teaching.

Method and way of working name the reusable way in this account. Practice may instead refer to learning exercises, performed work or a continuing shared practice; recover the relation under A.3.1 and A.6.P.WMR when it changes the interpretation. C.36.MC supplies the additional grounds for claims about shared culture and its transmission. A job can name assigned work or a position; appointment alone supplies neither a Method nor its mastery.

These positions can be occupied by people, AI systems and organizations where the action and authority permit it. A human may help an AI system recover relevant context; an AI may explain a task to a person; agents may compare interpretations and exchange results. An AI's first-person report does not by itself establish introspective access, durable memory or a human form of motivation. Check the action through the system and conditions that produce it.

A recipient is not necessarily the purchaser, employer, sponsor or downstream user. For a sponsored course, the employer may want reliable work, the person may want career development and the teacher may need a feasible practice commitment. Obtain a compatible undertaking or expose the disagreement. A sponsor's payment does not make every personal disclosure available to the sponsor. An employee's participation requirement does not establish a desire to learn. Legitimate duties, personal choices and the help arrangement need their own grounds.

#### Library.Reference:9.2.3 - Choose the form of help by the missing contribution

Choose the form of help by what needs to change in the work.

**Expert performance and a continuing service.** A qualified provider performs an operation or supplies its result. This is appropriate when the receiving work needs that result and personal acquisition is unnecessary, too costly or too late. Explain the input, response window, usable output and exception return. Establish who can supply the required input and who can obtain and use the result in the receiving work. Arrange help with any missing contribution. A permanent specialist service is a legitimate arrangement. Its dependence becomes a defect when it defeats the intended result, exceeds the agreed burden or leaves no viable continuation under a consequential interruption.

**Joint work.** Participants can be responsible to a common customer for different operations on the same result. Identify what each undertakes and how the operations fit together. This can account for their work as peers. To arrange help within it, establish whose result another participant intends to make obtainable. Working together can supply missing capacity. It can also let the participants work out what to build or how to interpret a result when they cannot yet specify separate orders. For someone learning the work, joint performance can make the decisive operation observable. When learning is intended, an expert can demonstrate, invite the learner to try the operation, compare attempts and return control. The learner's own attempt shows what they can do under the support then available.

**Teaching and coached practice.** When capability development is intended, the result concerns the learner's own capability under the required conditions. Choose explanation, demonstration, practice or feedback according to what the learner already does and what they need to learn. A helper can model a decision during teaching. To judge a learner’s own choice, use work in which the choice remained theirs under the intended conditions of support. Demonstration, explanation, exercises and workplace practice have different contributions. The Human Capability Development patterns supply the professional development arrangement, including preparation, motivation, feedback, transfer and retention. Obtain the missing contribution from a source or provider qualified for it. A performer can use a recording for timely feedback. Where the work needs a teacher who can observe the attempt and adapt the instruction, establish that teaching competence as well as knowledge of the subject.

**Mentoring and developmental guidance.** Developmental help can support choices about work, learning and professional opportunities. Identify the contribution needed: explanation, feedback on an attempt, access to other participants, comparison of feasible continuations or interpretation of experience. An experienced colleague, peers with different expertise or a suitable service can supply different parts. Retain the recipient's direction and the rights of each participant. A formal mentoring appointment can allocate time and duties; competence and the fit of the advice still need their own grounds. When the next continuation is uncertain, establish what is already attained and what alternatives are available under the present conditions, using A.22.CGUS:4.1. Use HCD.2 for a human capability-development programme comparison when that result is needed.

**Coaching and facilitation.** Some help enables a person or group to articulate a difficulty, examine assumptions, choose and coordinate its own action. Questions and dialogue can be appropriate when the participants possess or can obtain the relevant subject knowledge. They cannot substitute for a missing technical calculation or a required professional judgement. Group facilitation, implementation facilitation and a coaching profession have overlapping activities but different promises and qualifications. IS.3 addresses support for a target practice; its use does not qualify every professional coaching intervention.

**Leadership in participation.** A contribution may fail because the role is unclear, unattractive, unsupported or disputed. LDR.1 connects its purpose, the participant, conditions and legitimate undertaking. The leader can explain why the contribution matters, alter an infeasible role, provide conditions or accept a different allocation. Encouragement cannot create a missing resource, and labelling an objection as resistance does not answer it. Leadership may include a justified role assignment; it does not turn a coerced declaration of enthusiasm into evidence of commitment.

Teaching can support a needed learning contribution within leadership. Participation, agreement, preference, learning and performance remain different results. An informed objection or refusal can be warranted; persuasive success does not establish the participant's competence.

**Learning in marketing.** Finding prospective users, helping them evaluate an offer, purchase or use it, and helping users explain it to others can include learning. Identify what changes and for whom. A changed product preference may depend on new understanding without establishing mastery of a Method. A purchase need not involve that change, and a user need not be the purchaser. A recommendation or public account of use can supply contact or testimony without proving that another participant learned. MKT.Reference and MKT.Application:5 connect these contributions to the relevant marketing work. Apply the helping account to the recipient's wanted or legitimately undertaken result, not merely the seller's objective.

**Provision and condition repair.** Access, time, tools, decision rights, usable explanations and timely specialist help can make an already available capability effective. Restore the missing condition where that makes the work possible. If a separate learning need is already established, arrange the practice it requires. Where the effect of restoring access would change that choice, examine what the performer can then do. If several recipients repeatedly need the same provision, compare a shared service or platform with repeated individual help. Qualified judgement and local interpretation may remain necessary even when the common input is standardized.

A change of form is often the useful move. A team initially works alongside a specialist to discover what information matters, later uses a stable service, and learns a bounded operation when repeated use makes local capability worthwhile. Another team retains expert provision because rare, difficult cases do not justify broad training. For each transition, name the changed result, the new division of work and the conditions of continued use. Do not assume that every engagement must end in complete independence.

#### Library.Reference:9.2.4 - Make the engagement intelligible and revisable

Start from what the recipient needs next. A request can name both an aim and the recipient’s explanation of the difficulty. When that explanation is mistaken, show how the correction bears on the aim. A specialist entrusted with the choice of means can act on a better diagnosis within that mandate. If the change would displace a separately promised result, take it to whoever holds that decision. For a question deliberately posed under an assumption, reason under its terms. [Recovering the question (C.39.QR:4.1)](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/FPF-Spec.md#c39qr41---recover-the-question-independently-of-the-proposed-answer)

A result already in hand can meet that need even if the original provider is no longer available. For a fresh delivery, use an established service when its inputs, delivery time and output fit the receiving work. Its terms may already assign responsibility for inputs and exceptions. When the practice needs a new or changed support arrangement, use IS.3. Consult the recipient and providers about the knowledge, decisions or commitments needed from them. If their contributions still need to be worked out together, follow a real input through to the usable result and agree when to reconsider the arrangement.

When help concerns further learning, establish the current aim, attained contributions and present conditions before choosing the continuation. A.22.CGUS:4.1 helps determine which alternatives are available, blocked or still unknown. Availability does not by itself generate a worthwhile alternative or choose the best one. Use the subject Method and HCD.2 where programme comparison is needed. Retain achieved work when a changed condition requires revising only a dependent choice.

Keep these uses of curriculum and route wording distinct:

| What the account concerns | What it establishes |
| --- | --- |
| Curriculum or learning-product design | Intended learning contributions, content, tasks, assessment, provision, dependencies and alternatives for entrants. It need not prescribe one sequence for every learner. |
| A personal programme or plan | A selected, revisable continuation for a known learner under stated conditions and commitments. |
| Presently available continuations | Alternatives with their availability conditions; availability does not mean selection or completion. |
| A learning trajectory | The recorded history of attempts, help and supported changes for that learner, in the HCD.4 sense. |

A route can include justified sequencing, partial order and conditional branches. Its represented dependencies and choices determine what it permits. HCD.28 helps an author design a product for its intended entrants. HCD.2 helps establish feasibility or compare programmes for a known person. An authorized decision holder can use that result when a choice is due. HCD.27 supports recovery from a missed contribution. [UNESCO-UNEVOC's curriculum terminology comparison](https://connect.unevoc.unesco.org/home/TVETipedia+Glossary/lang=en/show=term/term=Curriculum) records several professional uses. Recover the particular claim rather than treating the word as a universal route prescription.

Suppose an analyst asks for help with a report. “Review it with me” can mean that the expert corrects this report, that the analyst learns to judge future reports, or that the pair develops a new review Method. If the request is ambiguous, a consequential defect in the report can help the analyst and the expert clarify which result is wanted. For a request limited to tomorrow's report, a usable correction is sufficient if the expert can supply it in time. If the analyst is to produce next month’s report without the expert, use relevant earlier work to decide what assistance is needed. Access to the right edition may suffice. If the analyst still needs to learn how to judge a defect, let them practise that judgement and give feedback. When a question about the criterion would change the commissioned result or its justification, obtain a competent interpretation or investigate the unresolved issue.

For simple help, an explicit exchange may suffice. For continuing services, clarify the class of requests, response conditions, responsibilities, access, escalation, costs and the way either party can seek revision. Keep the service promise distinct from the learner's capability promise and from an employer's role obligation.

Use the agreement to establish which act commits the provider and which act counts as acceptance. A request can activate an existing commitment. Proper delivery can itself count as acceptance on the agreed terms. When the terms call for a decision by an authorised recipient, the provider’s declaration alone leaves that decision open. Dietz and Mulder distinguish the production result from the coordination acts through which the participants request, promise, declare and accept it. The parties can discuss feasibility and can dispute the grounds of a claim or obligation. The account’s human social premises do not automatically apply to every employment relationship or technical agent. Engineering.Reference:21.3 supplies the Library's treatment of permissible continuation; ECO.5–6 supply economic relations where private information, incentives and dependence affect the arrangement.

Both parties can make errors, lack capability or act opportunistically. A provider can conceal inability or manufacture dependence. A principal can withhold promised inputs, change the acceptance criterion after delivery, use disclosed vulnerability against the recipient or refuse agreed compensation. Diagnose the actual action and evidence. More obedience does not repair an abusive demand, and attributing bad intent does not establish it.

When a party requests a change, determine what has already happened, which commitments remain and what cannot be undone. A cancelled analysis can leave expert time spent and a consequential decision already made. Agree a feasible continuation, compensation or termination within the actual authority. Where the authority itself is disputed, provide a route for a competent decision; another coaching session is not that decision.

#### Library.Reference:9.2.5 - Separate the helping episode from its continuing provider

One useful engagement does not establish how to provide help repeatedly. A continuing provider needs a way to recognize demand, maintain relevant expertise, make help available, protect time for its development and learn from recurring difficulties. Establish how recipients can reach the provider and what their commitments require during an interruption. They may be able to wait or stop. A promise with a fixed deadline may require a substitute, timely recovery or an agreed change.

A help episode can reveal several different improvements. A repeatedly missing input may justify a shared data service. A repeated interpretation error may call for a better explanation and practice. A slow response may reflect too much demand for scarce expertise. Contradictory answers may reveal a disputed Method. Return the findings to the authorized recipient. If the undertaking includes arranging a repair, identify who can act on the cause and what information may be shared with them. Creating a larger office does not by itself repair any of them.

Team Topologies contributes a useful distinction between collaboration, service provision and facilitation of another team's capability. Its original setting is software delivery. The distinction is useful here because those interactions produce different results and dependencies. A permanent provider can conduct temporary enabling engagements, and a capable team can still consume a continuing platform service. Neither an office's permanence nor its organizational chart determines the interaction for each request.

For example, a quality specialist first collaborates with production staff to establish a measurement procedure. The resulting calibration service can then become a recurring provision. A short training engagement can prepare local staff to recognize an exception and use the service correctly. These three contributions are connected. The quality culture additionally requires that people and agents continue to use, question and renew the procedure; section 9.3 explains that relation.

A platform can carry standardized knowledge, tools, access and routine operations. Arrange the maintenance its promised service requires. An exception may call for specialist judgement, or the service may end with a defined stop or referral. A template library can give a practitioner a form selected by a specialist or the means to choose one. Establish who selects the form and who can use it for the receiving work. The KCAE and implementation-facilitation contributions address these different questions; a download count answers neither.

#### Library.Reference:9.2.6 - Compare dedicated, common and distributed offices

Thematic breadth and organizational concentration are independent decisions. A narrow quality service can be distributed across plants. A broad development office can retain local specialists. An organization can centralize infrastructure while keeping professional judgement close to each receiving activity.

An office or centre of excellence is an organizational arrangement with a mandate, personnel or technical means, resources and service relations. Distinguish at least these functions when their allocation changes the result: perform specialist work; help others participate and learn; provide common infrastructure; develop professional Methods; maintain a repertoire; advise on standards; and make an authorized assurance or control judgement.

| Arrangement | When it can supply the result well | Main burden or failure to examine | A condition that can change the choice |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A dedicated thematic office | Demand repeatedly needs deep related expertise, a stable professional judgement or close contact with one practice | Idle rare capacity, separation from local work, duplication of common provision | Demand becomes too varied or too sparse to sustain the specialist service |
| A common office with professional directions | Several practices share intake, scheduling, infrastructure or development support while retaining qualified domain judgement | A generic front door can misdiagnose requests; pooled queues and competing priorities can erase local responsiveness | One direction needs materially different authority, response time or preparation |
| Distributed provision | Local context changes rapidly, local practitioners can supply much help and immediate access matters | Uneven quality, repeated invention, hidden peer workload, weak continuity when a specialist leaves | Repeated common problems justify a shared supplier or a scarce expert cannot be replicated |
| A hybrid with external specialists | Common provision and local action are both needed; rare expertise can be obtained across organizational boundaries | Coordination, access, handover and dependence on response commitments | The external provider cannot meet the receiving window or retain needed context |

Compare complete arrangements under C.32.MWA.PP and, where they must be developed together, C.40.JD. Include recipient preparation, local time, travel or context reconstruction, continuing maintenance, queues, coordination and exception handling. Shared preparation is counted once at the comparison boundary. Professional interpretation required for every case remains recurring demand. Saving the helper's time by shifting unexplained work to recipients is not a whole saving.

The office's name should follow its mandate. A PMO may provide scheduling tools, portfolio information, project expertise, development or assurance in different combinations. An agile office may support experimentation and coordination without owning every team's method choice. A literacy initiative needs to specify the literacy and the recipient's real tasks: understanding a maintenance instruction, evaluating a scientific claim and operating a digital tool have different content. A general support function can organize access and learning conditions, but it cannot substitute one subject competence for another.

Professional sources expose further differences that a common office must preserve. [RUSA's 2025 reference-service competencies](https://www.ala.org/rusa/resources/guidelines/professional) distinguish assistance with an information need from formal instruction, while connecting user collaboration, maintained resources and service development. [UNESCO's adult-learning recommendation](https://www.unesco.org/en/legal-affairs/recommendation-adult-learning-and-education) treats literacy within wider participation and flexible learning provision; a digital-skills course does not exhaust that scope. [NASA's engineering arrangements](https://nodis3.gsfc.nasa.gov/displayDir.cfm?Internal_ID=N_PR_7120_005F_&page_name=Chapter3) distinguish working-level engineering support from formally delegated technical authority and retain a route for dissent. These are different professional and institutional constructions. They support explicit allocation of service, learning and judgement; they do not establish a universal office design.

Domain sources describe particular arrangements, including contemporary AI centres of excellence. Their experience can supply mechanisms and useful contrasts; a vendor operating model does not establish the optimum for every institution.

#### Library.Reference:9.2.7 - Help and assurance can require different relations

Help often depends on exposing uncertainty and an unfinished attempt. An assurance judgement may need independence, a defined standard and an accountable acceptance decision. The same institution can provide both, but their authority, information use and conflicts must be clear.

A specialist who coached a team may have excellent knowledge of the work while lacking the independence required for a particular judgement. A learner may withhold difficulties if every coaching exchange automatically changes a performance rating. Conversely, a recipient may incorrectly expect confidential help to exempt an unsafe or otherwise impermissible action from the institution's actual duties.

Establish who receives which information, who makes the judgement and whether the applicable obligation permits the same performer to occupy both roles. Separate people, access or providers when the required independence cannot otherwise obtain. Do not promise confidentiality that the provider cannot maintain. The right separation depends on the work and governing obligation; a universal ban on sharing an office can waste scarce expertise without resolving the conflict.

The same question applies to AI assistance. A system that helped create an answer may also supply a useful check, but the same system's confidence is not independent evidence. Use a distinct basis or another qualified checker when the receiving claim needs one. Routine low-consequence help need not acquire a costly review ceremony.

#### Library.Reference:9.2.8 - Work through the capacity and participation together

Consider a constructed comparison for three teams introducing the same reporting practice. Each needs one specialist judgement a week for four weeks. A dedicated specialist engagement takes two hours per team per week: one hour of local preparation and one hour of discussion. The specialist has eight hours a week, of which two are committed to maintaining the service and handling exceptions.

The separate arrangement consumes six hours of specialist time each week and fits exactly beside the two continuing hours. It leaves no reserve for additional local difficulty. A proposed common clinic replaces the three preparation hours with one shared preparation hour, retains three one-hour team discussions and adds one hour of weekly coordination. Its request-related demand is five hours; together with continuing work it uses seven. The nominal saving is one specialist hour a week. It is a saving only if the shared preparation supplies the same relevant grounds and each team can provide its local information without hidden extra expert work.

The calculation selects a possible shared service, not yet an organizational unit. A dedicated reporting office can supply it when the specialist judgement and mandate need a stable home.
